<!DOCTYPE html><html lang="en"><head><meta charset="utf-8"><meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1"><script>
			(() => {
				const KEY = "ui-mode";
				const media = window.matchMedia("(prefers-color-scheme: dark)");
				const isPref = (v) => v === "light" || v === "dark" || v === "auto";

				const readPref = () => {
					try {
						const v = localStorage.getItem(KEY);
						return isPref(v) ? v : "auto";
					} catch {
						return "auto";
					}
				};

				const resolveMode = (pref) =>
					pref === "light" || pref === "dark"
						? pref
						: media.matches
							? "dark"
							: "light";

				const applyTheme = () => {
					const root = document.documentElement;
					const pref = readPref();
					const mode = resolveMode(pref);
					// data-mode drives Nimbus dark-mode CSS; data-theme is kept for
					// any ported component CSS that still keys off Starlight's attribute.
					if (mode === "dark") root.setAttribute("data-mode", "dark");
					else root.removeAttribute("data-mode");
					root.setAttribute("data-theme", mode);
					root.style.colorScheme = mode;
					// Drive the toggle icon from the root attribute (set here, pre-paint)
					// so it never flashes the SSR default before the toggle script loads.
					root.setAttribute("data-nb-pref", pref); // selected preference
					root.setAttribute("data-nb-state", mode); // resolved (light/dark)
				};

				applyTheme();
				document.addEventListener("astro:after-swap", applyTheme);
				// Live OS changes only move the page when the user is following the
				// system (auto). An explicit light/dark choice is left untouched.
				media.addEventListener("change", () => {
					if (readPref() === "auto") applyTheme();
				});

				// Cross-tab sync.
				window.addEventListener("storage", (e) => {
					if (e.key === KEY) applyTheme();
				});

				window.__nbApplyTheme = applyTheme;
			})();
		</script><style>

				display: block;
			}

	(() => {
		const root = document.documentElement;
		const isPreference = (value) => value === "wrangler" || value === "cf";
		const readStoredPreference = () => {
			try {
				const stored = localStorage.getItem("ui-cli-preference");
				return isPreference(stored) ? stored : undefined;
			} catch {
				return undefined;
			}
		};
		const readDefault = (pathname) =>
			pathname === "/cf" || pathname.startsWith("/cf/") ? "cf" : "wrangler";
		const resolvePreference = (pathname) => {
			if (isPreference(window.__nbCliPreferenceOverride)) {
				return window.__nbCliPreferenceOverride;
			}
			const stored = readStoredPreference();
			if (stored) return stored;
			return readDefault(pathname);
		};

		if (!root.dataset.nbCliPreference) {
			root.dataset.nbCliPreference = resolvePreference(location.pathname);
		}

		if (!window.__nbCliPreferenceBound) {
			document.addEventListener("astro:before-swap", (event) => {
				const newRoot = event.newDocument.documentElement;
				newRoot.dataset.nbCliPreference = resolvePreference(event.to.pathname);
			});
			window.__nbCliPreferenceBound = true;
		}

<p>The Agents SDK has a new <a href="/agents/models/"><code>createAI</code></a> function for the <a href="/agents/models/ai-sdk/">AI SDK</a> and <a href="/agents/models/pi-ai/">Pi AI</a>. It unifies what <code>workers-ai-provider</code> and <code>ai-gateway-provider</code> did: Workers AI models, AI Gateway catalog models, dynamic routes, and vendor models, through the <code>AI</code> binding or an account id and API token.</p>
<p><code>workers-ai-provider</code> and <code>ai-gateway-provider</code> are now feature frozen and marked as deprecated. Please migrate at your convenience.</p>

<span class="line"><span class="nb-shiki-1itgoe">import</span><span class="nb-shiki-140thh"> { streamText } </span><span class="nb-shiki-1itgoe">from</span><span class="nb-shiki-mdbnqw"> "ai"</span><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">;</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span class="nb-shiki-1itgoe">export</span><span class="nb-shiki-1itgoe"> default</span><span class="nb-shiki-140thh"> {</span></span>

<span class="line"><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">};</span></span></code></pre></figure></div><div role="tabpanel" data-nb-tabs-content data-nb-tab-label="TypeScript" class><figure class="nb-code-figure" data-nb-lang="ts"><pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark nb-shiki-c6xiwz" tabindex="0" data-language="ts" data-nb-lang="ts"><code><span class="line"><span class="nb-shiki-1itgoe">import</span><span class="nb-shiki-140thh"> { createAI } </span><span class="nb-shiki-1itgoe">from</span><span class="nb-shiki-mdbnqw"> "agents/models/ai-sdk"</span><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">;</span></span>

<p>With <code>createAI</code> you get:</p>
<ul>
<li><strong>Every model by id.</strong> Workers AI <code>@cf/</code> ids, any model in the <a href="/ai/models/">AI Gateway catalog</a>, and <a href="/ai-gateway/features/dynamic-routing/">dynamic routes</a> as <code>dynamic/&lt;route&gt;</code>.</li>
<li><strong>Vendor model objects.</strong> Pass a model from the vendor's own package, such as <code>ai(anthropic("claude-opus-5-5"))</code>. It goes unchanged to AI Gateway's <a href="/ai-gateway/usage/providers/">provider-native endpoint</a>.</li>
<li><strong>The binding, or an account id and API token.</strong> Use a gateway in another account, or call from outside a Worker.</li>
<li><strong>Gateway options, fallback, and BYOK.</strong> Caching, logging, metadata, retries, <code>byokAlias</code>, <code>zdr</code>, and <code>vendorKey</code>.</li>
<li><strong>Stream resume.</strong> Through the binding, a catalog or dynamic route stream that drops mid-answer resumes.</li>
<li><strong>Other model types.</strong> Embeddings, images, speech, transcription, and reranking in the AI SDK provider, and images in Pi AI.</li>
</ul>
<div class="table-scroll" tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="Table"><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Before</th>
<th>After</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><code>createWorkersAI({ binding })</code></td>
<td><code>createAI({ binding })</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>createWorkersAI({ accountId, apiKey })</code></td>
<td><code>createAI({ accountId, apiToken })</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>createAiGateway(...)</code></td>
<td><code>createAI(...)</code>, with <code>fallback</code> for a list of models</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>createAISearch</code> from <code>workers-ai-provider</code></td>
<td><code>createAISearchNamespace</code> from <code>ai-search-provider</code></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></div>
<p>For AI Search, use <a href="/ai-search/agent-sdks/ai-sdk/"><code>ai-search-provider</code></a>. For more information, refer to <a href="/agents/models/">Models</a>.</p></div></div></article><article class="nb-cl-entry relative pb-13 pl-[var(--body-x)] max-md:pb-11 max-md:pl-0"><div class="contents"><time datetime="2026-10-19" class="text-muted-foreground mb-2 block font-mono text-[0.8125rem] leading-[1.1] font-medium whitespace-nowrap tabular-nums lg:absolute lg:top-[var(--node-y)] lg:right-[calc(100%+1.75rem)] lg:mb-0 lg:w-[var(--date-w)] lg:-translate-y-1/2 lg:text-right">Oct 19, 2026</time><span class="nb-cl-dot" aria-hidden="true"></span></div><div class="max-w-[40rem]"><a href="/changelog/post/2026-10-19-clairic-workers-ai/" class="nb-cl-title-link group inline-block text-inherit no-underline"><h2 class="text-foreground m-0 text-[1.375rem] leading-[1.25] font-semibold tracking-[-0.02em] text-balance decoration-1 underline-offset-[3px] group-hover:underline">Introducing Clairic: an extraction model for typed values, now on Workers AI</h2></a><div class="mt-[0.6875rem]"><div class="flex flex-row flex-wrap gap-2"><a href="/workers-ai/" class="no-underline"><span class="inline-flex items-center rounded-full font-medium whitespace-nowrap bg-success-muted text-success px-2 py-0.5 text-xs">Workers AI</span></a></div></div><div class="docs-content nb-cl-prose mt-[1.125rem] max-w-none">
<p><a href="/workers-ai/models/clairic/"><code>@cf/cloudflare/clairic</code></a> is now available on Workers AI as a preview. Clairic is the first <a href="/workers-ai/concepts/extraction-models/">extraction model</a>: a System One model that answers with values instead of text. We intend to release the model with open weights soon.</p>
<p>Decision models such as <a href="/workers-ai/models/clef/">Clef</a> choose among options you define up front. Many structured-output tasks do not have answers you can list in advance: an invoice total, a delivery date, the number of days a shipment is late, or what a repair will cost. Clairic handles these. You send a state and a schema of typed fields, and it returns one typed value per field. A value is read from the input, computed from it, or predicted beyond it.</p>
<p>Clairic does not generate tokens. It reads your input in one pass and decodes each answer under the rules of its type, so every answer is a valid value of the type you asked for, or <code>null</code> when the input does not determine it.</p>
<div tabindex="-1" class="heading-wrapper level-h4"><h4 id="typed-fields">Typed fields</h4><a class="anchor-link" href="#typed-fields"><span aria-hidden="true" class="anchor-icon"><svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path fill="currentcolor" d="m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z"></path></svg></span></a></div>
<p>Define as many fields as you need. Fields that do not fit beside the input in one pass are split across parallel passes, so there is no upper limit.</p>
<div class="table-scroll" tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="Table"><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Type</th>
<th>Returns</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><code>text</code></td>
<td>An exact span of the input, with its character offsets</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>number</code></td>
<td>A number read or worked out from the input, honoring <code>integer</code> and <code>decimals</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>date</code>, <code>time</code>, <code>datetime</code></td>
<td>Real calendar dates and valid times, in ISO form</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>duration</code></td>
<td>A length of time in the unit you choose</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>list</code></td>
<td>Every matching text, number, or date, in input order</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>estimate</code></td>
<td>A distribution inside your <code>min</code> and <code>max</code>, on your <code>step</code> grid, on a linear or log scale</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>forecast</code></td>
<td>One estimate per future step of a series, up to 64 steps</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></div>
<p>Every scalar answer includes <code>p_null</code>, the probability that the input does not determine the value, and a <code>confidence</code> score. Estimates and forecasts include a median and an 80% interval (<code>p10</code> to <code>p90</code>). Set <code>options.include_quantiles</code> to also get all 99 percentiles. Use these to decide what to automate and what to send to review.</p>
<p>You can also pass up to four images alongside the state.</p>
<div><div data-nb-tabs data-nb-sync-key="workersExamples" class><div class="relative flex border-b border-border" role="tablist" data-nb-tabs-list><span class="bg-primary pointer-events-none absolute -bottom-px h-0.5 rounded-t-sm transition-[left,width] duration-200 ease-out" data-nb-tabs-indicator aria-hidden="true"></span></div><div class="mt-3"><div role="tabpanel" data-nb-tabs-content data-nb-tab-label="JavaScript" class><figure class="nb-code-figure" data-nb-lang="js"><pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark nb-shiki-c6xiwz" tabindex="0" data-language="js" data-nb-lang="js"><code><span class="line"><span class="nb-shiki-1itgoe">const</span><span class="nb-shiki-dzsirb"> response</span><span class="nb-shiki-1itgoe"> =</span><span class="nb-shiki-1itgoe"> await</span><span class="nb-shiki-140thh"> env.</span><span class="nb-shiki-dzsirb">AI</span><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">.</span><span class="nb-shiki-1t8gfj">run</span><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">(</span><span class="nb-shiki-mdbnqw">"@cf/cloudflare/clairic"</span><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">, {</span></span>

<p>The current endpoint is an early checkpoint, and we expect it to improve:</p>
<ul>
<li><strong>Arithmetic</strong>: One- and two-step calculations, such as totals, differences, conversions, and date arithmetic, are reliable. Long chains of dependent steps and aggregations across many table rows are not yet. Extract the inputs with Clairic and do the math in code.</li>
<li><strong>Estimates</strong>: Clairic estimates best when the input contains evidence such as comparables, history, or descriptive text. Without evidence, the interval is wide.</li>
<li><strong>Forecasts</strong>: Forecasting is basic. Next-step forecasts follow recent patterns, but forecasts several steps out flatten toward the average, and Clairic does not anticipate events the series does not show, such as holidays. It does not replace a time-series model trained on your data.</li>
<li><strong>Calibration</strong>: Intervals are trained to be calibrated and are on most held-out sets, but they can run narrow on unfamiliar data.</li>
<li><strong>Confidence</strong>: Every answer is well formed, not necessarily correct. Use <code>confidence</code>, <code>p_null</code>, and the intervals to decide what to automate and what to send to review.</li>
<li><strong>Dates</strong>: Dates without a year are resolved from context. If a field depends on the current date, set <code>reference_date</code> or add a line such as <code>Today: 2026-10-19</code> to the state.</li>
</ul>
<div tabindex="-1" class="heading-wrapper level-h4"><h4 id="pricing">Pricing</h4><a class="anchor-link" href="#pricing"><span aria-hidden="true" class="anchor-icon"><svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path fill="currentcolor" d="m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z"></path></svg></span></a></div>
<p>Clairic costs $0.10 per million input tokens. Because it generates no tokens, there is no output token charge.</p>
<div tabindex="-1" class="heading-wrapper level-h4"><h4 id="get-started">Get started</h4><a class="anchor-link" href="#get-started"><span aria-hidden="true" class="anchor-icon"><svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path fill="currentcolor" d="m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z"></path></svg></span></a></div>
<p>Use Clairic through the <a href="/workers-ai/configuration/bindings/">Workers AI binding</a> (<code>env.AI.run()</code>) or the REST API at <code>/ai/run</code>. You can also use <a href="/ai-gateway/">AI Gateway</a> with these endpoints.</p>
<p>For more information, refer to the <a href="/workers-ai/models/clairic/">Clairic model page</a>, <a href="/workers-ai/concepts/extraction-models/">extraction models</a>, <a href="/workers-ai/platform/pricing/">pricing</a>, and the <a href="https://blog.cloudflare.com/clairic-extraction-model/" target="_blank" rel="noopener">blog post<span class="external-link"> ↗︎</span></a>.</p></div></div></article><article class="nb-cl-entry relative pb-13 pl-[var(--body-x)] max-md:pb-11 max-md:pl-0"><div class="contents"><time datetime="2026-10-19" class="text-muted-foreground mb-2 block font-mono text-[0.8125rem] leading-[1.1] font-medium whitespace-nowrap tabular-nums lg:absolute lg:top-[var(--node-y)] lg:right-[calc(100%+1.75rem)] lg:mb-0 lg:w-[var(--date-w)] lg:-translate-y-1/2 lg:text-right">Oct 19, 2026</time><span class="nb-cl-dot" aria-hidden="true"></span></div><div class="max-w-[40rem]"><a href="/changelog/post/2026-10-19-deepseek-v4.1-flash-workers-ai/" class="nb-cl-title-link group inline-block text-inherit no-underline"><h2 class="text-foreground m-0 text-[1.375rem] leading-[1.25] font-semibold tracking-[-0.02em] text-balance decoration-1 underline-offset-[3px] group-hover:underline">DeepSeek V4.1 Flash 1017 now available on Workers AI</h2></a><div class="mt-[0.6875rem]"><div class="flex flex-row flex-wrap gap-2"><a href="/workers-ai/" class="no-underline"><span class="inline-flex items-center rounded-full font-medium whitespace-nowrap bg-success-muted text-success px-2 py-0.5 text-xs">Workers AI</span></a></div></div><div class="docs-content nb-cl-prose mt-[1.125rem] max-w-none"><p><a href="/workers-ai/models/deepseek-v4.1-flash-1017/"><code>@cf/deepseek-ai/deepseek-v4.1-flash-1017</code></a> is now available on Workers AI. DeepSeek V4.1 Flash is a multimodal Mixture-of-Experts model with 552B backbone parameters. It activates 8B parameters per token during prefill and 16B during decode, which keeps input-heavy agentic workloads fast and low-cost.</p>
<p>The 1017 release is an update to DeepSeek V4.1 Flash with improvements across reasoning, coding, agentic tasks, and visual benchmarks, and it completes tasks with shorter outputs.</p>
<p><strong>Key capabilities:</strong></p>
<ul>
<li><strong>Vision</strong>: Send images alongside text in the same request.</li>
<li><strong>Reasoning</strong>: Reasoning is on by default at <code>high</code> effort. Set <code>reasoning_effort</code> to <code>low</code> or <code>max</code> to change the effort, or to <code>none</code> to turn reasoning off.</li>
<li><strong>Function calling</strong>: Build agents that invoke tools and APIs across multiple conversation turns.</li>
<li><strong>Long context</strong>: A full 1,048,576 token context window.</li>
</ul>
<p>DeepSeek V4.1 Flash requires the <a href="/workers/platform/pricing/#workers">Workers Paid plan</a> or prepaid <a href="/ai-gateway/features/unified-billing/">AI Gateway credits</a>. The default rate limit is 20 requests per minute with standard Workers AI billing and 50 requests per minute with prepaid AI Gateway credits. For details, refer to <a href="/workers-ai/platform/limits/#paid-models">limits</a>.</p>
<p>Use DeepSeek V4.1 Flash through the <a href="/workers-ai/configuration/bindings/">Workers AI binding</a> (<code>env.AI.run()</code>), the REST API, the <a href="/workers-ai/configuration/open-ai-compatibility/">OpenAI-compatible endpoint</a>, or <a href="/ai-gateway/">AI Gateway</a>.</p>
<p>For more information, refer to the <a href="/workers-ai/models/deepseek-v4.1-flash-1017/">DeepSeek V4.1 Flash model page</a> and <a href="/workers-ai/platform/pricing/">pricing</a>.</p></div></div></article><article class="nb-cl-entry relative pb-13 pl-[var(--body-x)] max-md:pb-11 max-md:pl-0"><div class="contents"><time datetime="2026-10-15" class="text-muted-foreground mb-2 block font-mono text-[0.8125rem] leading-[1.1] font-medium whitespace-nowrap tabular-nums lg:absolute lg:top-[var(--node-y)] lg:right-[calc(100%+1.75rem)] lg:mb-0 lg:w-[var(--date-w)] lg:-translate-y-1/2 lg:text-right">Oct 15, 2026</time><span class="nb-cl-dot" aria-hidden="true"></span></div><div class="max-w-[40rem]"><a href="/changelog/post/2026-10-15-phonellm-mini-workers-ai/" class="nb-cl-title-link group inline-block text-inherit no-underline"><h2 class="text-foreground m-0 text-[1.375rem] leading-[1.25] font-semibold tracking-[-0.02em] text-balance decoration-1 underline-offset-[3px] group-hover:underline">Pipecat PhoneLLM Mini now available on Workers AI</h2></a><div class="mt-[0.6875rem]"><div class="flex flex-row flex-wrap gap-2"><a href="/workers-ai/" class="no-underline"><span class="inline-flex items-center rounded-full font-medium whitespace-nowrap bg-success-muted text-success px-2 py-0.5 text-xs">Workers AI</span></a><a href="/realtime/" class="no-underline"><span class="inline-flex items-center rounded-full font-medium whitespace-nowrap bg-success-muted text-success px-2 py-0.5 text-xs">Realtime</span></a></div></div><div class="docs-content nb-cl-prose mt-[1.125rem] max-w-none">
<p><a href="/workers-ai/models/phonellm-mini-alpha-2/"><code>@cf/daily-co/phonellm-mini-alpha-2</code></a>, a model from the <a href="https://www.pipecat.ai/" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Pipecat<span class="external-link"> ↗︎</span></a> team at Daily, is now available on <a href="/workers-ai/">Workers AI</a>. PhoneLLM is built for voice agents and other realtime AI applications, where a model has to reply quickly and call the right tool mid-conversation.</p>
<p>In a voice agent, the language model sits between speech-to-text and text-to-speech, so every second it takes adds to the caller's wait. Developers usually choose between a fast model without reasoning mode, which can miss instructions or tool calls, and a slower model in reasoning mode, which handles both well. Pipecat post-trained NVIDIA Nemotron 3 Nano 30B on phone and customer support scenarios so PhoneLLM follows instructions and calls tools better without reasoning mode.</p>
<p>You can use PhoneLLM through the <a href="/workers-ai/configuration/bindings/">Workers AI binding</a> (<code>env.AI.run()</code>) or the <a href="/workers-ai/get-started/rest-api/">REST API</a> at <code>/ai/run</code>. Pipecat recommends <code>temperature: 0</code>. Also set <code>chat_template_kwargs: { enable_thinking: false }</code>.</p>
<div tabindex="-1" class="heading-wrapper level-h4"><h4 id="run-a-voice-agent-with-pipecat-on-workers">Run a voice agent with Pipecat on Workers</h4><a class="anchor-link" href="#run-a-voice-agent-with-pipecat-on-workers"><span aria-hidden="true" class="anchor-icon"><svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path fill="currentcolor" d="m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z"></path></svg></span></a></div>
<p><a href="https://docs.pipecat.ai/" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Pipecat<span class="external-link"> ↗︎</span></a> is an open-source Python framework for building voice agents. Now that <a href="/workers/languages/python/">Python Workers</a> are generally available, you can run a Pipecat voice agent on Cloudflare.</p>
<p>Our <a href="https://github.com/cloudflare/realtime-examples/tree/main/pipecat-voice-agent" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Pipecat voice agent example<span class="external-link"> ↗︎</span></a> runs in a <a href="/durable-objects/">Durable Object</a> and uses WebRTC as the voice transport through the <a href="/realtime/sfu/">Realtime SFU</a>. It uses <a href="/workers-ai/models/deepseek-v4-flash-0731/">DeepSeek V4 Flash</a> by default. To deploy it with PhoneLLM, give this prompt to your coding agent:</p>

<p>For more information, refer to the <a href="/workers-ai/models/phonellm-mini-alpha-2/">PhoneLLM Mini model page</a>, <a href="https://huggingface.co/pipecat-ai/phonellm-mini-alpha-2" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Pipecat's model card<span class="external-link"> ↗︎</span></a>, and <a href="/workers-ai/platform/pricing/">Workers AI pricing</a>.</p></div></div></article><article class="nb-cl-entry relative pb-13 pl-[var(--body-x)] max-md:pb-11 max-md:pl-0"><div class="contents"><time datetime="2026-10-09" class="text-muted-foreground mb-2 block font-mono text-[0.8125rem] leading-[1.1] font-medium whitespace-nowrap tabular-nums lg:absolute lg:top-[var(--node-y)] lg:right-[calc(100%+1.75rem)] lg:mb-0 lg:w-[var(--date-w)] lg:-translate-y-1/2 lg:text-right">Oct 9, 2026</time><span class="nb-cl-dot" aria-hidden="true"></span></div><div class="max-w-[40rem]"><a href="/changelog/post/2026-10-09-clef-omni-workers-ai/" class="nb-cl-title-link group inline-block text-inherit no-underline"><h2 class="text-foreground m-0 text-[1.375rem] leading-[1.25] font-semibold tracking-[-0.02em] text-balance decoration-1 underline-offset-[3px] group-hover:underline">Clef-omni adds audio and video input, Clef-flash is now cheaper, and Clef is faster</h2></a><div class="mt-[0.6875rem]"><div class="flex flex-row flex-wrap gap-2"><a href="/workers-ai/" class="no-underline"><span class="inline-flex items-center rounded-full font-medium whitespace-nowrap bg-success-muted text-success px-2 py-0.5 text-xs">Workers AI</span></a></div></div><div class="docs-content nb-cl-prose mt-[1.125rem] max-w-none">
<p><a href="/workers-ai/models/clef-omni/"><code>@cf/cloudflare/clef-omni</code></a> is now available on Workers AI. Clef-omni is a decision model that takes audio (WAV or MP3) and video (MP4 or WebM) input alongside text and images. It joins <a href="/workers-ai/models/clef/">Clef</a> and <a href="/workers-ai/models/clef-flash/">Clef-flash</a> in the Clef family of open-weight decision models. We also cut the price of Clef-flash, so it now costs less than Jev, and made Clef faster.</p>
<div tabindex="-1" class="heading-wrapper level-h4"><h4 id="clef-omni-one-decision-model-for-every-modality">Clef-omni: one decision model for every modality</h4><a class="anchor-link" href="#clef-omni-one-decision-model-for-every-modality"><span aria-hidden="true" class="anchor-icon"><svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path fill="currentcolor" d="m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z"></path></svg></span></a></div>
<p>Previously, making a decision about a voice recording or a video meant chaining models together: transcribe the speech, split the audio and visual tracks, then pass the results to a text decision model. Clef-omni reads every modality directly in one request. A video's soundtrack is aligned with its frames, so the model can reason over what is seen and heard at the same time.</p>
<p>Clef-omni is built on a 30B-parameter mixture-of-experts (MoE) backbone with 3B active parameters. Like the rest of the Clef family, it does not generate text. It scores every allowed answer in a single pass, so decisions return quickly:</p>
<ul>
<li>Text requests: about 20 ms</li>
<li>Image or audio inputs: under 100 ms</li>
<li>A 21-second video clip with sound: about 300 ms</li>
</ul>
<p>Pass media as base64 data URLs in the <code>images</code>, <code>audio</code>, and <code>videos</code> fields:</p>
<div><div data-nb-tabs data-nb-sync-key="workersExamples" class><div class="relative flex border-b border-border" role="tablist" data-nb-tabs-list><span class="bg-primary pointer-events-none absolute -bottom-px h-0.5 rounded-t-sm transition-[left,width] duration-200 ease-out" data-nb-tabs-indicator aria-hidden="true"></span></div><div class="mt-3"><div role="tabpanel" data-nb-tabs-content data-nb-tab-label="JavaScript" class><figure class="nb-code-figure" data-nb-lang="js"><pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark nb-shiki-c6xiwz" tabindex="0" data-language="js" data-nb-lang="js"><code><span class="line"><span class="nb-shiki-1itgoe">const</span><span class="nb-shiki-dzsirb"> response</span><span class="nb-shiki-1itgoe"> =</span><span class="nb-shiki-1itgoe"> await</span><span class="nb-shiki-140thh"> env.</span><span class="nb-shiki-dzsirb">AI</span><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">.</span><span class="nb-shiki-1t8gfj">run</span><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">(</span><span class="nb-shiki-mdbnqw">"@cf/cloudflare/clef-omni"</span><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">, {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">	model: </span><span class="nb-shiki-mdbnqw">"clef-omni"</span><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">	state:</span></span>
<span class="line"><span class="nb-shiki-mdbnqw">		"Review the installation: a photo of the unit, an audio recording of it running, and a video of the fan."</span><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">	images: [</span><span class="nb-shiki-mdbnqw">"data:image/png;base64,&#x3C;base64-png>"</span><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">],</span></span>
<span class="line"><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">	audio: [</span><span class="nb-shiki-mdbnqw">"data:audio/mpeg;base64,&#x3C;base64-mp3>"</span><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">],</span></span>
<span class="line"><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">	videos: [</span><span class="nb-shiki-mdbnqw">"data:video/mp4;base64,&#x3C;base64-mp4>"</span><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">],</span></span>
<span class="line"><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">	questions: {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">		label_visible: {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">			type: </span><span class="nb-shiki-mdbnqw">"noul"</span><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">			instructions:</span></span>
<span class="line"><span class="nb-shiki-mdbnqw">				"Is the model and serial number label visible in the photo?"</span><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">		},</span></span>
<span class="line"><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">		sounds_normal: {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">			type: </span><span class="nb-shiki-mdbnqw">"noul"</span><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">			instructions:</span></span>
<span class="line"><span class="nb-shiki-mdbnqw">				"Does the unit sound like it is running smoothly, without rattling or grinding?"</span><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">		},</span></span>
<span class="line"><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">		fan_running: {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">			type: </span><span class="nb-shiki-mdbnqw">"noul"</span><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">			instructions: </span><span class="nb-shiki-mdbnqw">"Is the fan running in the video?"</span><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">		},</span></span>
<span class="line"><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">	},</span></span>
<span class="line"><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">});</span></span></code></pre></figure></div><div role="tabpanel" data-nb-tabs-content data-nb-tab-label="TypeScript" class><figure class="nb-code-figure" data-nb-lang="ts"><pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark nb-shiki-c6xiwz" tabindex="0" data-language="ts" data-nb-lang="ts"><code><span class="line"><span class="nb-shiki-1itgoe">const</span><span class="nb-shiki-dzsirb"> response</span><span class="nb-shiki-1itgoe"> =</span><span class="nb-shiki-1itgoe"> await</span><span class="nb-shiki-140thh"> env.</span><span class="nb-shiki-dzsirb">AI</span><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">.</span><span class="nb-shiki-1t8gfj">run</span><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">(</span><span class="nb-shiki-mdbnqw">"@cf/cloudflare/clef-omni"</span><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">, {</span></span>

<div tabindex="-1" class="heading-wrapper level-h4"><h4 id="clef-flash-is-now-cheaper">Clef-flash is now cheaper</h4><a class="anchor-link" href="#clef-flash-is-now-cheaper"><span aria-hidden="true" class="anchor-icon"><svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path fill="currentcolor" d="m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z"></path></svg></span></a></div>
<p>Clef-flash now costs <strong>$0.038 per million input tokens</strong>, down from $0.090, which makes it cheaper than Jev. To offer this price, the hosted Clef-flash context window is now 24K tokens, down from 64K. Based on usage data, only 0.24% of requests exceed 24K input tokens. If you need a larger context window, use Clef, which keeps its 64K context window.</p>
<p>The Clef-flash weights on Hugging Face are unchanged and support up to a 256K context window if you self-host.</p>

For details on how each input type is tokenized, refer to the <a href="/workers-ai/models/clef/">Clef</a>, <a href="/workers-ai/models/clef-flash/">Clef-flash</a>, and <a href="/workers-ai/models/clef-omni/">Clef-omni</a> model pages.</p>
<div tabindex="-1" class="heading-wrapper level-h4"><h4 id="clef-is-now-faster">Clef is now faster</h4><a class="anchor-link" href="#clef-is-now-faster"><span aria-hidden="true" class="anchor-icon"><svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path fill="currentcolor" d="m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z"></path></svg></span></a></div>
<p>We optimized how Clef is served on Workers AI, so it now returns decisions up to 2x faster. The model weights are unchanged.</p>

<p>Part of this speedup comes from moving Clef to <a href="https://github.com/sgl-project/sglang" target="_blank" rel="noopener">SGLang<span class="external-link"> ↗︎</span></a>. Clef support is coming to SGLang in version 0.5.22 (<a href="https://github.com/sgl-project/sglang/pull/42721" target="_blank" rel="noopener">PR #42721<span class="external-link"> ↗︎</span></a>). If you self-host Clef, launch commands are available in the <a href="https://huggingface.co/collections/Cloudflare/clef" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Clef collection on Hugging Face<span class="external-link"> ↗︎</span></a>.</p>
<div tabindex="-1" class="heading-wrapper level-h4"><h4 id="get-started">Get started</h4><a class="anchor-link" href="#get-started"><span aria-hidden="true" class="anchor-icon"><svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path fill="currentcolor" d="m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z"></path></svg></span></a></div>
<p>Clef-omni follows the same System One API as Clef and Clef-flash, and works with <a href="/ai-gateway/">AI Gateway</a>. To try it, change the model ID to <code>@cf/cloudflare/clef-omni</code> and set the <code>model</code> selector to <code>clef-omni</code>.</p>
<p>For more information, refer to the <a href="/workers-ai/models/clef-omni/">Clef-omni model page</a> and <a href="/workers-ai/platform/pricing/">pricing</a>.</p></div></div></article><article class="nb-cl-entry relative pb-13 pl-[var(--body-x)] max-md:pb-11 max-md:pl-0"><div class="contents"><time datetime="2026-10-01" class="text-muted-foreground mb-2 block font-mono text-[0.8125rem] leading-[1.1] font-medium whitespace-nowrap tabular-nums lg:absolute lg:top-[var(--node-y)] lg:right-[calc(100%+1.75rem)] lg:mb-0 lg:w-[var(--date-w)] lg:-translate-y-1/2 lg:text-right">Oct 1, 2026</time><span class="nb-cl-dot" aria-hidden="true"></span></div><div class="max-w-[40rem]"><a href="/changelog/post/2026-10-01-clef-workers-ai/" class="nb-cl-title-link group inline-block text-inherit no-underline"><h2 class="text-foreground m-0 text-[1.375rem] leading-[1.25] font-semibold tracking-[-0.02em] text-balance decoration-1 underline-offset-[3px] group-hover:underline">Introducing Clef: Cloudflare&#39;s first open-source decision models, now on Workers AI</h2></a><div class="mt-[0.6875rem]"><div class="flex flex-row flex-wrap gap-2"><a href="/workers-ai/" class="no-underline"><span class="inline-flex items-center rounded-full font-medium whitespace-nowrap bg-success-muted text-success px-2 py-0.5 text-xs">Workers AI</span></a></div></div><div class="docs-content nb-cl-prose mt-[1.125rem] max-w-none">
<p>Meet <a href="/workers-ai/models/clef/"><code>@cf/cloudflare/clef</code></a> and <a href="/workers-ai/models/clef-flash/"><code>@cf/cloudflare/clef-flash</code></a>, the first models trained by the Cloudflare Workers AI team, available on Workers AI today.</p>
<p>Clef is a decision model, in the same family as <a href="https://typesafe.ai/blog/introducing-system-one-models-and-jev" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Typesafe's Jev<span class="external-link"> ↗︎</span></a>. Instead of generating text, it reads an input state and a set of typed questions, then returns a probability for every allowed answer. Your agent gets a structured decision it can act on immediately, for example: route the ticket, block the request, or escalate to a human. There is no free-form output to parse and no reasoning tokens to wait for.</p>
<p>Both models are hosted on Workers AI as <a href="/workers-ai/models/clef/">Clef</a> and <a href="/workers-ai/models/clef-flash/">Clef-flash</a>. We are also open-sourcing the weights under the Apache 2.0 license on Hugging Face: <a href="https://huggingface.co/Cloudflare/clef" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Clef<span class="external-link"> ↗︎</span></a> and <a href="https://huggingface.co/Cloudflare/clef-flash" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Clef-flash<span class="external-link"> ↗︎</span></a>. Read the <a href="https://blog.cloudflare.com/clef-decision-models/" target="_blank" rel="noopener">launch blog post<span class="external-link"> ↗︎</span></a> for the full story, including how we trained them.</p>
<p>We are also launching a reinforcement learning (RL) fine-tuning service to help you tune Clef for your own workloads. <a href="https://www.cloudflare.com/resource/clef-rl-interest" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Sign up to work with us as a design partner<span class="external-link"> ↗︎</span></a>.</p>
<div tabindex="-1" class="heading-wrapper level-h4"><h4 id="built-for-the-hot-path">Built for the hot path</h4><a class="anchor-link" href="#built-for-the-hot-path"><span aria-hidden="true" class="anchor-icon"><svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path fill="currentcolor" d="m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z"></path></svg></span></a></div>
<p>Clef is designed to be fast so decisions come back in milliseconds. Across our 43 benchmark runs, we achieved speeds where Clef is 2.5x faster than Jev at the median, and Clef-flash 13x faster.</p>
<div class="table-scroll" tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="Table"><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Latency</th>
<th>Clef</th>
<th>Clef-flash</th>
<th>Jev</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td>Median</td>
<td>209.3 ms</td>
<td><strong>38.8 ms</strong></td>
<td>524.1 ms</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>p95</td>
<td>238.6 ms</td>
<td><strong>122.4 ms</strong></td>
<td>536.0 ms</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></div>
<p>Hosting on Workers AI adds to that speed. Requests run on GPUs across Cloudflare's network, running close to your users, so the network round trip stays short. You can put Clef directly in the request path of your agent, then hand off to an LLM on Workers AI to take action.</p>
<div tabindex="-1" class="heading-wrapper level-h4"><h4 id="leading-the-benchmarks">Leading the benchmarks</h4><a class="anchor-link" href="#leading-the-benchmarks"><span aria-hidden="true" class="anchor-icon"><svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path fill="currentcolor" d="m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z"></path></svg></span></a></div>

<p>On Typesafe's own workflow evals, Clef beats Jev in 3 of 4 areas: invoice processing, customer service, and security incidents. The full results are on the <a href="https://huggingface.co/Cloudflare/clef" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Hugging Face model card<span class="external-link"> ↗︎</span></a>.</p>
<div tabindex="-1" class="heading-wrapper level-h4"><h4 id="drop-in-compatible-with-jev">Drop-in compatible with Jev</h4><a class="anchor-link" href="#drop-in-compatible-with-jev"><span aria-hidden="true" class="anchor-icon"><svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path fill="currentcolor" d="m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z"></path></svg></span></a></div>
<div class="table-scroll" tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="Table"><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Model</th>
<th>Size</th>
<th>Best for</th>
<th>Context window</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><a href="/workers-ai/models/clef/"><code>@cf/cloudflare/clef</code></a></td>
<td>27B</td>
<td>Highest-precision decisions</td>
<td>64K tokens</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/workers-ai/models/clef-flash/"><code>@cf/cloudflare/clef-flash</code></a></td>
<td>9B</td>
<td>Latency-critical, hot-path decisions</td>
<td>64K tokens</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></div>
<p>Clef follows the System One API, so you can switch an existing Jev integration to Clef by changing the endpoint and model. Ask up to 64 questions per request, in three types:</p>
<ul>
<li><strong><code>noul</code></strong>: A yes/no question. Returns the probability that the answer is yes.</li>
<li><strong><code>choice</code></strong>: Pick one option from a set you define. Returns the chosen option, a probability per option, and a confidence value.</li>
<li><strong><code>score</code></strong>: Rate against an ordered rubric. Returns a probability-weighted score and a probability per level.</li>
</ul>
<div><div data-nb-tabs data-nb-sync-key="workersExamples" class><div class="relative flex border-b border-border" role="tablist" data-nb-tabs-list><span class="bg-primary pointer-events-none absolute -bottom-px h-0.5 rounded-t-sm transition-[left,width] duration-200 ease-out" data-nb-tabs-indicator aria-hidden="true"></span></div><div class="mt-3"><div role="tabpanel" data-nb-tabs-content data-nb-tab-label="JavaScript" class><figure class="nb-code-figure" data-nb-lang="js"><pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark nb-shiki-c6xiwz" tabindex="0" data-language="js" data-nb-lang="js"><code><span class="line"><span class="nb-shiki-1itgoe">const</span><span class="nb-shiki-dzsirb"> response</span><span class="nb-shiki-1itgoe"> =</span><span class="nb-shiki-1itgoe"> await</span><span class="nb-shiki-140thh"> env.</span><span class="nb-shiki-dzsirb">AI</span><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">.</span><span class="nb-shiki-1t8gfj">run</span><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">(</span><span class="nb-shiki-mdbnqw">"@cf/cloudflare/clef"</span><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">, {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">	model: </span><span class="nb-shiki-mdbnqw">"clef"</span><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">	state: </span><span class="nb-shiki-mdbnqw">"Checkout has been failing for every customer for the last hour."</span><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">,</span></span>

<span class="line"><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">		},</span></span>
<span class="line"><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">		team: {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">			type: </span><span class="nb-shiki-mdbnqw">"choice"</span><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">,</span></span>

<span class="line"><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">			},</span></span>

<span class="line"><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">	},</span></span>

<span class="line"><span class="nb-shiki-21nrsd">// response.answers.urgent.noul -> probability the request is urgent</span></span>
<span class="line"><span class="nb-shiki-21nrsd">// response.answers.team.choice -> highest-probability team</span></span></code></pre></figure></div><div role="tabpanel" data-nb-tabs-content data-nb-tab-label="TypeScript" class><figure class="nb-code-figure" data-nb-lang="ts"><pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark nb-shiki-c6xiwz" tabindex="0" data-language="ts" data-nb-lang="ts"><code><span class="line"><span class="nb-shiki-1itgoe">const</span><span class="nb-shiki-dzsirb"> response</span><span class="nb-shiki-1itgoe"> =</span><span class="nb-shiki-1itgoe"> await</span><span class="nb-shiki-140thh"> env.</span><span class="nb-shiki-dzsirb">AI</span><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">.</span><span class="nb-shiki-1t8gfj">run</span><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">(</span><span class="nb-shiki-mdbnqw">"@cf/cloudflare/clef"</span><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">, {</span></span>

<div tabindex="-1" class="heading-wrapper level-h4"><h4 id="what-you-can-build-with-decision-models">What you can build with decision models</h4><a class="anchor-link" href="#what-you-can-build-with-decision-models"><span aria-hidden="true" class="anchor-icon"><svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path fill="currentcolor" d="m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z"></path></svg></span></a></div>
<ul>
<li><strong>Support triage</strong>: Decide whether a ticket is urgent and which team owns it, then route it without a human in the loop.</li>
<li><strong>Threat intelligence</strong>: Classify a website by category. Paired with <a href="/browser-run/">Browser Run</a>, Clef fetched, rendered, and classified a domain in 2.2 seconds, compared to 4.7 seconds for <code>gpt-oss-120b</code> in the same workflow.</li>
<li><strong>Trust and safety</strong>: Score user submissions against your own policy rubric and act on the probability.</li>
<li><strong>Agent guardrails</strong>: Let an agent check "should I take this action?" in tens of milliseconds before calling a tool.</li>
<li><strong>Visual classification</strong>: Pass up to four images alongside the state. Unlike text-only decision models, Clef has a vision encoder.</li>
</ul>
<div tabindex="-1" class="heading-wrapper level-h4"><h4 id="get-started">Get started</h4><a class="anchor-link" href="#get-started"><span aria-hidden="true" class="anchor-icon"><svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path fill="currentcolor" d="m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z"></path></svg></span></a></div>
<p>Use Clef through the <a href="/workers-ai/configuration/bindings/">Workers AI binding</a> (<code>env.AI.run()</code>) or the REST API at <code>/ai/run</code>. You can also use <a href="/ai-gateway/">AI Gateway</a> with these endpoints.</p>
<p>For more information, refer to the <a href="/workers-ai/models/clef/">Clef model page</a>, the <a href="/workers-ai/models/clef-flash/">Clef-flash model page</a>, and <a href="/workers-ai/platform/pricing/">pricing</a>.</p></div></div></article><article class="nb-cl-entry relative pb-13 pl-[var(--body-x)] max-md:pb-11 max-md:pl-0"><div class="contents"><time datetime="2026-09-17" class="text-muted-foreground mb-2 block font-mono text-[0.8125rem] leading-[1.1] font-medium whitespace-nowrap tabular-nums lg:absolute lg:top-[var(--node-y)] lg:right-[calc(100%+1.75rem)] lg:mb-0 lg:w-[var(--date-w)] lg:-translate-y-1/2 lg:text-right">Sep 17, 2026</time><span class="nb-cl-dot" aria-hidden="true"></span></div><div class="max-w-[40rem]"><a href="/changelog/post/2026-09-17-reject-if-busy/" class="nb-cl-title-link group inline-block text-inherit no-underline"><h2 class="text-foreground m-0 text-[1.375rem] leading-[1.25] font-semibold tracking-[-0.02em] text-balance decoration-1 underline-offset-[3px] group-hover:underline">Reject busy synchronous inference requests</h2></a><div class="mt-[0.6875rem]"><div class="flex flex-row flex-wrap gap-2"><a href="/workers-ai/" class="no-underline"><span class="inline-flex items-center rounded-full font-medium whitespace-nowrap bg-success-muted text-success px-2 py-0.5 text-xs">Workers AI</span></a></div></div><div class="docs-content nb-cl-prose mt-[1.125rem] max-w-none">
<p>The <code>rejectIfBusy</code> option lets synchronous Workers AI inference requests fail when capacity is unavailable. Use it when your application should not wait in a capacity queue.</p>
<p>Pass the option as the third argument to the Workers AI binding:</p>
<div><div data-nb-tabs data-nb-sync-key="workersExamples" class><div class="relative flex border-b border-border" role="tablist" data-nb-tabs-list><span class="bg-primary pointer-events-none absolute -bottom-px h-0.5 rounded-t-sm transition-[left,width] duration-200 ease-out" data-nb-tabs-indicator aria-hidden="true"></span></div><div class="mt-3"><div role="tabpanel" data-nb-tabs-content data-nb-tab-label="JavaScript" class><figure class="nb-code-figure" data-nb-lang="js"><pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark nb-shiki-c6xiwz" tabindex="0" data-language="js" data-nb-lang="js"><code><span class="line"><span class="nb-shiki-1itgoe">const</span><span class="nb-shiki-dzsirb"> response</span><span class="nb-shiki-1itgoe"> =</span><span class="nb-shiki-1itgoe"> await</span><span class="nb-shiki-140thh"> env.</span><span class="nb-shiki-dzsirb">AI</span><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">.</span><span class="nb-shiki-1t8gfj">run</span><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">(</span></span>
<span class="line"><span class="nb-shiki-mdbnqw">	"@cf/google/gemma-4-26b-a4b-it"</span><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">	{</span></span>
<span class="line"><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">		messages: [{ role: </span><span class="nb-shiki-mdbnqw">"user"</span><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">, content: </span><span class="nb-shiki-mdbnqw">"Explain capacity queues."</span><span class="nb-shiki-140thh"> }],</span></span>
<span class="line"><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">	},</span></span>
<span class="line"><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">	{ rejectIfBusy: </span><span class="nb-shiki-dzsirb">true</span><span class="nb-shiki-140thh"> },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">);</span></span></code></pre></figure></div><div role="tabpanel" data-nb-tabs-content data-nb-tab-label="TypeScript" class><figure class="nb-code-figure" data-nb-lang="ts"><pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark nb-shiki-c6xiwz" tabindex="0" data-language="ts" data-nb-lang="ts"><code><span class="line"><span class="nb-shiki-1itgoe">const</span><span class="nb-shiki-dzsirb"> response</span><span class="nb-shiki-1itgoe"> =</span><span class="nb-shiki-1itgoe"> await</span><span class="nb-shiki-140thh"> env.</span><span class="nb-shiki-dzsirb">AI</span><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">.</span><span class="nb-shiki-1t8gfj">run</span><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">(</span></span>
<span class="line"><span class="nb-shiki-mdbnqw">	"@cf/google/gemma-4-26b-a4b-it"</span><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">	{</span></span>
<span class="line"><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">		messages: [{ role: </span><span class="nb-shiki-mdbnqw">"user"</span><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">, content: </span><span class="nb-shiki-mdbnqw">"Explain capacity queues."</span><span class="nb-shiki-140thh"> }],</span></span>
<span class="line"><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">	},</span></span>
<span class="line"><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">	{ rejectIfBusy: </span><span class="nb-shiki-dzsirb">true</span><span class="nb-shiki-140thh"> },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">);</span></span></code></pre></figure></div></div></div></div>
<p>For the native REST API, add the option to the request body:</p>
<figure class="nb-code-figure" data-nb-lang="bash"><pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark nb-shiki-c6xiwz" tabindex="0" data-language="bash" data-nb-lang="bash"><code><span class="line"><span class="nb-shiki-1t8gfj">curl</span><span class="nb-shiki-dzsirb"> --request</span><span class="nb-shiki-mdbnqw"> POST</span><span class="nb-shiki-dzsirb"> \</span></span>
<span class="line"><span class="nb-shiki-dzsirb">  --url</span><span class="nb-shiki-mdbnqw"> "https://api.cloudflare.com/client/v4/accounts/</span><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">$ACCOUNT_ID</span><span class="nb-shiki-mdbnqw">/ai/run/@cf/google/gemma-4-26b-a4b-it"</span><span class="nb-shiki-dzsirb"> \</span></span>
<span class="line"><span class="nb-shiki-dzsirb">  --header</span><span class="nb-shiki-mdbnqw"> "Authorization: Bearer </span><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">$CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN</span><span class="nb-shiki-mdbnqw">"</span><span class="nb-shiki-dzsirb"> \</span></span>
<span class="line"><span class="nb-shiki-dzsirb">  --header</span><span class="nb-shiki-mdbnqw"> "Content-Type: application/json"</span><span class="nb-shiki-dzsirb"> \</span></span>
<span class="line"><span class="nb-shiki-dzsirb">  --data</span><span class="nb-shiki-mdbnqw"> '{</span></span>
<span class="line"><span class="nb-shiki-mdbnqw">    "messages": [{ "role": "user", "content": "Explain capacity queues." }],</span></span>
<span class="line"><span class="nb-shiki-mdbnqw">    "options": { "rejectIfBusy": true }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span class="nb-shiki-mdbnqw">  }'</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p>Refer to <a href="/workers-ai/features/reject-if-busy/">Reject busy requests</a> for OpenAI-compatible usage and error behavior.</p></div></div></article><article class="nb-cl-entry relative pb-13 pl-[var(--body-x)] max-md:pb-11 max-md:pl-0"><div class="contents"><time datetime="2026-08-28" class="text-muted-foreground mb-2 block font-mono text-[0.8125rem] leading-[1.1] font-medium whitespace-nowrap tabular-nums lg:absolute lg:top-[var(--node-y)] lg:right-[calc(100%+1.75rem)] lg:mb-0 lg:w-[var(--date-w)] lg:-translate-y-1/2 lg:text-right">Aug 28, 2026</time><span class="nb-cl-dot" aria-hidden="true"></span></div><div class="max-w-[40rem]"><a href="/changelog/post/2026-08-28-glm-5.3-workers-ai/" class="nb-cl-title-link group inline-block text-inherit no-underline"><h2 class="text-foreground m-0 text-[1.375rem] leading-[1.25] font-semibold tracking-[-0.02em] text-balance decoration-1 underline-offset-[3px] group-hover:underline">Z.ai GLM-5.3 now available on Workers AI</h2></a><div class="mt-[0.6875rem]"><div class="flex flex-row flex-wrap gap-2"><a href="/workers-ai/" class="no-underline"><span class="inline-flex items-center rounded-full font-medium whitespace-nowrap bg-success-muted text-success px-2 py-0.5 text-xs">Workers AI</span></a></div></div><div class="docs-content nb-cl-prose mt-[1.125rem] max-w-none"><p><a href="/workers-ai/models/glm-5.3/"><code>@cf/zai-org/glm-5.3</code></a> is now available on Workers AI. It is Z.ai's flagship agentic coding model, built for long-running, tool-driven development workflows rather than single-turn chat.</p>
<p>GLM-5.3 uses the same base model as GLM-5.2, with every gain coming from post-training. The results are substantial on coding and agentic benchmarks: <a href="https://huggingface.co/zai-org/GLM-5.3" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Z.ai reports<span class="external-link"> ↗︎</span></a> a 50% improvement over GLM-5.2 on its in-house Z.ai Code Bench, and calls GLM-5.3 the most capable open-weights model for coding. On public benchmarks, it scores 88.2 on Terminal Bench 2.1 (up from 81.0), 28.3 on Terminal Bench 3.0 — open-source state of the art, up from 4.6 — 66.9 on DeepSWE (up from 46.2), 78.1 on FrontierSWE (up from 67.5), and 42.5 on SWE-Marathon (up from 19.4). It is also the top-scoring model in Z.ai's comparisons on CyberGym for vulnerability discovery (84.5) and on long-horizon automation tasks like AutomationBench (48.2).</p>
<p>The price-to-performance ratio is the compelling part. On Workers AI, GLM-5.3 costs the same as GLM-5.2 — $1.40 per M input tokens, $0.26 per M cached input tokens, and $4.40 per M output tokens — while roughly doubling GLM-5.2's scores on long-horizon benchmarks like SWE-Marathon, and improving them by more than 6x on Terminal Bench 3.0.</p>
<p>GLM-5.3 requires the <a href="/workers/platform/pricing/#workers">Workers Paid plan</a> or prepaid <a href="/ai-gateway/features/unified-billing/">AI Gateway credits</a>.</p>
<p>Use GLM-5.3 through the <a href="/workers-ai/configuration/bindings/">Workers AI binding</a> (<code>env.AI.run()</code>), the REST API, the <a href="/workers-ai/configuration/open-ai-compatibility/">OpenAI-compatible endpoint</a>, or <a href="/ai-gateway/">AI Gateway</a>.</p>
<p>For more information, refer to the <a href="/workers-ai/models/glm-5.3/">GLM-5.3 model page</a> and <a href="/workers-ai/platform/pricing/">pricing</a>.</p></div></div></article><article class="nb-cl-entry relative pb-13 pl-[var(--body-x)] max-md:pb-11 max-md:pl-0"><div class="contents"><time datetime="2026-08-26" class="text-muted-foreground mb-2 block font-mono text-[0.8125rem] leading-[1.1] font-medium whitespace-nowrap tabular-nums lg:absolute lg:top-[var(--node-y)] lg:right-[calc(100%+1.75rem)] lg:mb-0 lg:w-[var(--date-w)] lg:-translate-y-1/2 lg:text-right">Aug 26, 2026</time><span class="nb-cl-dot" aria-hidden="true"></span></div><div class="max-w-[40rem]"><a href="/changelog/post/2026-08-26-glm-5.3-flash-workers-ai/" class="nb-cl-title-link group inline-block text-inherit no-underline"><h2 class="text-foreground m-0 text-[1.375rem] leading-[1.25] font-semibold tracking-[-0.02em] text-balance decoration-1 underline-offset-[3px] group-hover:underline">Z.ai GLM-5.3 Flash now available on Workers AI</h2></a><div class="mt-[0.6875rem]"><div class="flex flex-row flex-wrap gap-2"><a href="/workers-ai/" class="no-underline"><span class="inline-flex items-center rounded-full font-medium whitespace-nowrap bg-success-muted text-success px-2 py-0.5 text-xs">Workers AI</span></a></div></div><div class="docs-content nb-cl-prose mt-[1.125rem] max-w-none"><p><a href="/workers-ai/models/glm-5.3-flash/"><code>@cf/zai-org/glm-5.3-flash</code></a> is now available on Workers AI. It is the first natively multimodal model in the GLM-5 series, built on a Mixture-of-Experts architecture with 320B total parameters and 18B active per token.</p>
<p>GLM-5.3 Flash is the first GLM-family model on Workers AI to support multimodal inputs. It outperforms GLM-5.2 across benchmarks and real-world workloads at a lower price, while approaching Claude Opus 4.8 on coding and agentic benchmarks.</p>
<p>GLM-5.3 Flash requires the <a href="/workers/platform/pricing/#workers">Workers Paid plan</a> or prepaid <a href="/ai-gateway/features/unified-billing/">AI Gateway credits</a>.</p>
<p>Use GLM-5.3 Flash through the <a href="/workers-ai/configuration/bindings/">Workers AI binding</a> (<code>env.AI.run()</code>), the REST API, the <a href="/workers-ai/configuration/open-ai-compatibility/">OpenAI-compatible endpoint</a>, or <a href="/ai-gateway/">AI Gateway</a>.</p>
<p>For more information, refer to the <a href="/workers-ai/models/glm-5.3-flash/">GLM-5.3 Flash model page</a> and <a href="/workers-ai/platform/pricing/">pricing</a>.</p></div></div></article><article class="nb-cl-entry relative pb-13 pl-[var(--body-x)] max-md:pb-11 max-md:pl-0"><div class="contents"><time datetime="2026-08-17" class="text-muted-foreground mb-2 block font-mono text-[0.8125rem] leading-[1.1] font-medium whitespace-nowrap tabular-nums lg:absolute lg:top-[var(--node-y)] lg:right-[calc(100%+1.75rem)] lg:mb-0 lg:w-[var(--date-w)] lg:-translate-y-1/2 lg:text-right">Aug 17, 2026</time><span class="nb-cl-dot" aria-hidden="true"></span></div><div class="max-w-[40rem]"><a href="/changelog/post/2026-08-17-qwen-3.8-27b-workers-ai/" class="nb-cl-title-link group inline-block text-inherit no-underline"><h2 class="text-foreground m-0 text-[1.375rem] leading-[1.25] font-semibold tracking-[-0.02em] text-balance decoration-1 underline-offset-[3px] group-hover:underline">Qwen 3.8 27B now available on Workers AI</h2></a><div class="mt-[0.6875rem]"><div class="flex flex-row flex-wrap gap-2"><a href="/workers-ai/" class="no-underline"><span class="inline-flex items-center rounded-full font-medium whitespace-nowrap bg-success-muted text-success px-2 py-0.5 text-xs">Workers AI</span></a></div></div><div class="docs-content nb-cl-prose mt-[1.125rem] max-w-none"><p><a href="/workers-ai/models/qwen3.8-27b/"><code>@cf/qwen/qwen3.8-27b</code></a> is now available on Workers AI.</p>
<p>Qwen 3.8 27B is a 27-billion-parameter instruction-tuned vision language model from Alibaba's Qwen family. It processes images and text together, with reasoning and function calling for agentic workflows.</p>
<p><strong>Key capabilities:</strong></p>
<ul>
<li><strong>Vision</strong>: Accept image and text inputs and generate text responses.</li>
<li><strong>Reasoning</strong>: Support thinking mode for complex, step-by-step problem-solving.</li>
<li><strong>Function calling</strong>: Build agents that invoke tools and APIs across multiple conversation turns.</li>
<li><strong>262,144 token context window</strong>: Retain long conversations and multimodal inputs across extended agent sessions.</li>
</ul>
<p>Use Qwen 3.8 27B through the <a href="/workers-ai/configuration/bindings/">Workers AI binding</a> (<code>env.AI.run()</code>) or the REST API at <code>/ai/run</code>. You can also use <a href="/ai-gateway/">AI Gateway</a> with these endpoints.</p>
<p>For more information, refer to the <a href="/workers-ai/models/qwen3.8-27b/">Qwen 3.8 27B model page</a> and <a href="/workers-ai/platform/pricing/">pricing</a>.</p></div></div></article><article class="nb-cl-entry relative pb-13 pl-[var(--body-x)] max-md:pb-11 max-md:pl-0"><div class="contents"><time datetime="2026-08-14" class="text-muted-foreground mb-2 block font-mono text-[0.8125rem] leading-[1.1] font-medium whitespace-nowrap tabular-nums lg:absolute lg:top-[var(--node-y)] lg:right-[calc(100%+1.75rem)] lg:mb-0 lg:w-[var(--date-w)] lg:-translate-y-1/2 lg:text-right">Aug 14, 2026</time><span class="nb-cl-dot" aria-hidden="true"></span></div><div class="max-w-[40rem]"><a href="/changelog/post/2026-08-14-deepseek-v4-workers-ai/" class="nb-cl-title-link group inline-block text-inherit no-underline"><h2 class="text-foreground m-0 text-[1.375rem] leading-[1.25] font-semibold tracking-[-0.02em] text-balance decoration-1 underline-offset-[3px] group-hover:underline">DeepSeek V4 Flash and Pro now available on Workers AI</h2></a><div class="mt-[0.6875rem]"><div class="flex flex-row flex-wrap gap-2"><a href="/workers-ai/" class="no-underline"><span class="inline-flex items-center rounded-full font-medium whitespace-nowrap bg-success-muted text-success px-2 py-0.5 text-xs">Workers AI</span></a></div></div><div class="docs-content nb-cl-prose mt-[1.125rem] max-w-none"><p><a href="/workers-ai/models/deepseek-v4-pro-0813/"><code>@cf/deepseek-ai/deepseek-v4-pro-0813</code></a> and <a href="/workers-ai/models/deepseek-v4-flash-0731/"><code>@cf/deepseek-ai/deepseek-v4-flash-0731</code></a> are now available on Workers AI.</p>
<p>DeepSeek V4 Flash and DeepSeek V4 Pro are the first Workers AI models with a full <strong>one million (1,048,576) token context window</strong>. Use them for long-horizon agentic workflows, large codebases, and multi-step reasoning that exceed the context limits of every other model hosted on the platform.</p>
<p>DeepSeek V4 Flash is the faster, lower-cost sibling. This release supersedes the preview version with substantially enhanced agentic capabilities.</p>
<p><strong>Key capabilities:</strong></p>
<ul>
<li><strong>Reasoning</strong>: Both models support thinking mode for complex, step-by-step problem-solving.</li>
<li><strong>Function calling</strong>: Build agents that invoke tools and APIs across multiple conversation turns.</li>
<li><strong>Long context</strong>: Both models support a full 1,048,576 token context window.</li>
</ul>
<p>Both models require the <a href="/workers/platform/pricing/#workers">Workers Paid plan</a> or prepaid <a href="/ai-gateway/features/unified-billing/">AI Gateway credits</a>.</p>
<p>Use these models through the <a href="/workers-ai/configuration/bindings/">Workers AI binding</a> (<code>env.AI.run()</code>), the REST API, the <a href="/workers-ai/configuration/open-ai-compatibility/">OpenAI-compatible endpoint</a>, or <a href="/ai-gateway/">AI Gateway</a>.</p>
<p>For more information, refer to the <a href="/workers-ai/models/deepseek-v4-pro-0813/">DeepSeek V4 Pro model page</a>, the <a href="/workers-ai/models/deepseek-v4-flash-0731/">DeepSeek V4 Flash model page</a>, and <a href="/workers-ai/platform/pricing/">pricing</a>.</p></div></div></article><article class="nb-cl-entry relative pb-13 pl-[var(--body-x)] max-md:pb-11 max-md:pl-0"><div class="contents"><time datetime="2026-08-07" class="text-muted-foreground mb-2 block font-mono text-[0.8125rem] leading-[1.1] font-medium whitespace-nowrap tabular-nums lg:absolute lg:top-[var(--node-y)] lg:right-[calc(100%+1.75rem)] lg:mb-0 lg:w-[var(--date-w)] lg:-translate-y-1/2 lg:text-right">Aug 7, 2026</time><span class="nb-cl-dot" aria-hidden="true"></span></div><div class="max-w-[40rem]"><a href="/changelog/post/2026-08-07-workers-ai-unified-billing/" class="nb-cl-title-link group inline-block text-inherit no-underline"><h2 class="text-foreground m-0 text-[1.375rem] leading-[1.25] font-semibold tracking-[-0.02em] text-balance decoration-1 underline-offset-[3px] group-hover:underline">Workers AI and AI Gateway unify model access and billing</h2></a><div class="mt-[0.6875rem]"><div class="flex flex-row flex-wrap gap-2"><a href="/ai-gateway/" class="no-underline"><span class="inline-flex items-center rounded-full font-medium whitespace-nowrap bg-success-muted text-success px-2 py-0.5 text-xs">AI Gateway</span></a><a href="/workers-ai/" class="no-underline"><span class="inline-flex items-center rounded-full font-medium whitespace-nowrap bg-success-muted text-success px-2 py-0.5 text-xs">Workers AI</span></a></div></div><div class="docs-content nb-cl-prose mt-[1.125rem] max-w-none">
<p>Workers AI and AI Gateway now provide a unified path for accessing models and managing inference traffic. Use the same AI binding and REST API to call models hosted on Workers AI or by supported third-party providers, with AI Gateway providing observability, logging, caching, security, and billing controls.</p>
<div tabindex="-1" class="heading-wrapper level-h4"><h4 id="unified-entrypoints-and-observability">Unified entrypoints and observability</h4><a class="anchor-link" href="#unified-entrypoints-and-observability"><span aria-hidden="true" class="anchor-icon"><svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path fill="currentcolor" d="m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z"></path></svg></span></a></div>
<p>The <a href="/ai-gateway/usage/worker-binding-methods/">AI binding</a> supports both Workers AI and third-party models through <code>env.AI.run()</code>. The <a href="/ai-gateway/usage/rest-api/">REST API</a> provides shared <code>/ai/</code> endpoints with Cloudflare authentication across providers.</p>
<p>Route a Workers AI request through AI Gateway by specifying a gateway ID. Use <code>default</code> to automatically create a gateway on the first authenticated request, or specify an existing gateway to separate applications and workloads:</p>
<div><div data-nb-tabs data-nb-sync-key="workersExamples" class><div class="relative flex border-b border-border" role="tablist" data-nb-tabs-list><span class="bg-primary pointer-events-none absolute -bottom-px h-0.5 rounded-t-sm transition-[left,width] duration-200 ease-out" data-nb-tabs-indicator aria-hidden="true"></span></div><div class="mt-3"><div role="tabpanel" data-nb-tabs-content data-nb-tab-label="JavaScript" class><figure class="nb-code-figure" data-nb-lang="js"><pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark nb-shiki-c6xiwz" tabindex="0" data-language="js" data-nb-lang="js"><code><span class="line"><span class="nb-shiki-1itgoe">const</span><span class="nb-shiki-dzsirb"> response</span><span class="nb-shiki-1itgoe"> =</span><span class="nb-shiki-1itgoe"> await</span><span class="nb-shiki-140thh"> env.</span><span class="nb-shiki-dzsirb">AI</span><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">.</span><span class="nb-shiki-1t8gfj">run</span><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">(</span></span>

<span class="line"><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">		messages: [{ role: </span><span class="nb-shiki-mdbnqw">"user"</span><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">, content: </span><span class="nb-shiki-mdbnqw">"What is the capital of France?"</span><span class="nb-shiki-140thh"> }],</span></span>
